- **positive-interaction-ratio**: 当你在做团队管理、绩效辅导、1:1沟通，或感受到团队士气低落、成员抱怨时，激活此技能。 当你听到"下属不够自信"、"总是需要我盯着"、"员工说感受不到认可"时，主动激活。 不适用于：危机公关（紧急止损）、纯任务派发式管理（不需要情感连接）、流水线操作工管理。 关键触发信号：团队离职率上升、1:1变成吐槽会、批评后无改进、员工"不主动汇报问题"。 ([ace3000chao/book2startup](https://github.com/ace3000chao/book2startup/tree/HEAD/ScalingUp-skills/positive-interaction-ratio) · ★100)
- **positive-ion-mode-spectra-handling**: Use when re-analyzing positive ion mode LC–MS/MS metabolomics data and you need MZmine3 feature tables plus SIRIUS formula/adduct assignments (e.g., replicating the cultured-vs-conventional meat study). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/positive-ion-mode-spectra-handling))
- **positive-mentions-harvester**: Find and check praise for a person or firm. Use when reviews, press, talks or kind words are spread across sources. - **positive-mode-ionization-analysis**: Use when you have LC-MS metabolomics data in positive ionization mode and have already performed XCMS feature detection and RAMClustR clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/positive-mode-ionization-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **positive-negative-class-balancing**: Use when when training a formula rescoring model on MS/MS spectra where positive examples (correct molecular formulas) are unevenly distributed across molecular formula groups or vastly outnumbered by negative examples (incorrect candidates), resulting in class imbalance that degrades model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/positive-negative-class-balancing) · 2 copies)
- **positive-negative-interaction-partitioning**: Use when FT-ICR MS molecular intensity data and microbial abundance tables are available and the goal is to test how positive vs. negative DOM-microbe interactions respond to an environmental treatment such as warming. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/positive-negative-interaction-partitioning))
- **positive-negative-marker-scoring**: Use when clusters of closely related cell subtypes (e.g. naive vs effector T cells, immature B vs plasma cells) collapse to near-equal scores under positive-only markers, and negative marker evidence is needed to resolve them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/positive-negative-marker-scoring))
- **positive-negative-neutral-judge**: Use when the user wants positive/negative/neutral cross-check, triad deliberation ([UnlimitedAbandonWare/AbandonWareAi](https://github.com/UnlimitedAbandonWare/AbandonWareAi/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/positive-negative-neutral-judge) · ★1)
- **positive-negative-pair-generation**: Use when constructing an augmented training corpus for a Siamese/CNN compound-identification model from a library of pure-component spectra: pairs of spectra with matching vs. differing compound compositions must be synthesized before training pSCNN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/positive-negative-pair-generation))
- **positive-negative-sample-balancing**: Use when building benchmark datasets for a tissue- or species-specific site-prediction model and you need equal positive and negative sequence counts in both training and testing partitions to avoid class-imbalance bias. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/positive-negative-sample-balancing))
- **positive-negative-set-contrast-analysis**: Use when you have two balanced sets of centered, fixed-length nucleotide fragments (e.g., 5,298 vs 5,298 A. thaliana; 4,563 vs 4,563 M. musculus) and need to test whether the positive class shows position-specific residue enrichment or motifs relative to the negative class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/positive-negative-set-contrast-analysis))
- **positive-negative-variant-labeling**: Use when constructing supervised training data for non-coding variant prioritization, i.e. pairing known functional/eQTL/disease SNPs (positives) with frequency-matched common background SNVs (negatives) in tab-delimited variant files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/positive-negative-variant-labeling))

- **positive-population-preservation-quantification**: Use when benchmarking an MTI normalization method on single-cell feature data and you need to verify that biological signal fidelity — the fraction of marker-positive cells — is preserved between local and global thresholding strategies.
